NAME: supabase_execute_sql
ARGUMENTS: {'query': "CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION generate_insert_statements_any(p_schema_name text, p_table_name text)\nRETURNS text AS $$\nDECLARE\n  result text := '';\n  row_data record;\n  col_data record;\n  cols text;\n  vals text;\n  val text;\n  col_type text;\n  row_count int := 0;\nBEGIN\n  SELECT string_agg(quote_ident(c.column_name), ', ' ORDER BY c.ordinal_position)\n  INTO cols\n  FROM information_schema.columns c\n  WHERE c.table_schema = p_schema_name AND c.table_name = p_table_name;\n\n  IF cols IS NULL THEN\n    RETURN '-- No columns found for ' || p_schema_name || '.' || p_table_name || E'\\n';\n  END IF;\n\n  result := result || format(E'\\n-- Table: %s.%s\\n', p_schema_name, p_table_name);\n\n  FOR row_data IN EXECUTE format('SELECT row_to_json(t) AS row FROM %I.%I t', p_schema_name, p_table_name)\n  LOOP\n    row_count := row_count + 1;\n    vals := '';\n    FOR col_data IN\n      SELECT c.column_name, c.data_type, c.udt_name\n      FROM information_schema.columns c\n      WHERE c.table_schema = p_schema_name AND c.table_name = p_table_name\n      ORDER BY c.ordinal_position\n    LOOP\n      IF vals <> '' THEN vals := vals || ', '; END IF;\n\n      val := row_data.row->>col_data.column_name;\n\n      IF val IS NULL THEN\n        vals := vals || 'NULL';\n      ELSE\n        IF col_data.data_type = 'ARRAY' THEN\n          col_type := regexp_replace(col_data.udt_name, '^_(.*)$', '\\1[]');\n        ELSIF col_data.data_type = 'USER-DEFINED' THEN\n          col_type := col_data.udt_name;\n        ELSE\n          col_type := col_data.data_type;\n        END IF;\n        vals := vals || quote_literal(val) || '::' || col_type;\n      END IF;\n    END LOOP;\n\n    result := result || format('INSERT INTO %I.%I (%s) VALUES (%s) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;', p_schema_name, p_table_name, cols, vals) || E'\\n';\n  END LOOP;\n\n  IF row_count = 0 THEN\n    result := result || format('-- Table %s.%s is empty\\n', p_schema_name, p_table_name);\n  ELSE\n    result := result || format(E'-- End of %s.%s (%s rows)\\n', p_schema_name, p_table_name, row_count);\n  END IF;\n\n  RETURN result;\nEND;\n$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;", 'appId': 'app-cjmldrzgvw1t', 'userId': 'user-cjml2dkttc74', 'appType': 'Web'}

<observation>
SkillObservation(content='Base directory for this skill: /workspace/app-cjmldrzgvw1t/.skills/supabase-server\n\n# Supabase Backend\n\nApplies to all frontend stacks (Web / MiniProgram / App). Covers Supabase server-side Schema, RLS, Edge Functions, Storage, Realtime, and Auth configuration.\n\n- For **WeChat Mini-Program/Weapp**, read `references/miniprogram.md` (weapp-specific backend rules: persistence, anon RLS guard, remote-asset transfer, QR code generation).\n\n**Timezone**: Supabase runs in **UTC** by default. All `timestamptz` values are stored in UTC, `now()` returns UTC, and `pg_cron` schedules are interpreted in UTC. Convert to the user\'s local timezone in the frontend or via `AT TIME ZONE` in SQL when display/scheduling requires a specific offset.\n\n## Initial Setup\n\n- Use `supabase_init` to initialize the database or start it from a paused state\n\n## Schema Design\n\n- Minimize tables and columns; merge functionalities into single tables when possible\n- Set database-level default values for required fields\n  - Example: `owner_id uuid not null default auth.uid()`\n  - Example: `order_number text UNIQUE NOT NULL` — use a trigger bound to insert logic to auto-generate the order number\n- For multi-table relations, define them clearly to avoid missing link fields\n- ENUM values require explicit casts\n- **NEVER interact with `supabase/migrations/` directory** (including ls, cat, view)\n- Always apply changes through incremental migrations\n- Verify database state by querying table structure using `supabase_execute_sql`\n- Try to combine tasks and execute all SQL in a single run\n- Always consider performance and speed optimization\n- Disallow SQL statements with silent or ambiguous success semantics (e.g., `IF NOT EXISTS`, `IF EXISTS`) — all operations must fail explicitly when schema or data deviates from expectations\n\n## Data Insertion\n\n**Must-Add:**\n- System-required data (e.g., categories, form dropdown options)\n- **For image URLs**: Use `image_search` to get real URLs — **NEVER** use placeholder URLs like `https://placeholder-image.jpg`\n- Add initial seed data (INSERT INTO) in the migration SQL, using `image_search` for real image URLs\n\n**Allowed:**\n- Homepage display data (e.g., showcases, course previews, products)\n- Demo data (e.g., sample exam sets)\n\n**Forbidden:**\n- User data\n- User activity history (e.g., lottery, orders)\n- Real personal names or any privacy-related info\n\n## SQL Modification\n\n- If you have full context in memory, prioritize using it directly\n- If unsure about current data structure, query it first; do not rely on migration files\n- Under no circumstances should you view or modify migration files\n- If frontend requires a feature without a corresponding table, policy, or edge function, you may add it afterward\n- When modifying business data (non-DDL, policy, RPC, etc.), use `supabase_execute_sql`\n- Before writing api.ts, read the corresponding `supabase/migrations/*.sql` to confirm NOT NULL constraints for every column — context compression may lose field details; migrations are the ground truth\n\n## Security Policies (RLS)\n\n### Design Principles\n\n- **Every table MUST have RLS enabled** — no exceptions, regardless of whether login auth exists\n- **Full coverage per table**: Explicitly define policies for every combination of SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE × {anon, authenticated, admin}. **Never leave any blank** — missing policies silently return empty data or 0 affected rows\n  - Per-table review checklist: anon × {S/I/U/D}, authenticated × {S/I/U/D}, admin × {S/I/U/D}\n  - Anticipate future features: add corresponding constraints now for role×operation combinations that may be needed later\n- **INSERT always requires a matching SELECT**: Any role granted INSERT permission MUST also have a SELECT policy defined\n  - Example: If a policy "allows anyone to submit", add one so "submitters can view their own submissions"\n- Frontend and backend permissions must be aligned\n  - No permission, no interaction\n  - All submit-capable forms must have explicit, role-based permissions\n- PostgREST silently returns 0 affected rows (no error) when UPDATE/DELETE does not match RLS — writes appear to succeed but nothing changed. Tables the frontend needs to mutate must have explicit UPDATE/DELETE policies defined\n- Clear role boundaries\n  - Admins must have full read/write access\n  - Roles must have complete and secure permissions defined for every possible interaction scenario\n- All internal checks must use `SECURITY DEFINER`\n\n### Implementation Notes\n\n- RLS should only depend on **static columns or secure functions**\n- **Policy self-loop**: A policy\'s USING clause must not query the same table it protects — use a `SECURITY DEFINER` helper function instead (it bypasses RLS when querying the table)\n- Use public views to expose read-only access to selected fields of high-privilege tables\n- **The column checked by `auth.uid()` MUST auto-fill**: For each INSERT policy whose WITH CHECK contains `<col> = auth.uid()`, that exact `<col>` MUST be declared `NOT NULL DEFAULT auth.uid()`. The client never sends it. Apply this to that one column only — never blanket-default other uuid/FK columns (e.g. `assigned_to`), which would be a bug.\n  - ❌ `owner_id uuid NOT NULL` + client `.insert(payload)` → owner is NULL → "new row violates row-level security policy"\n  - ✅ `owner_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT auth.uid()`\n\n## Storage Buckets\n\n- Create for image/file uploads only\n- **CRITICAL**: NEVER store images/videos as Base64 in database\n- ALL image/video assets MUST be uploaded to Supabase Storage\n- When third-party APIs return image/video URLs, transfer to Storage in Edge Function\n- buckets must be created in migration SQL via `supabase_apply_migration` — **do not** create them dynamically inside Edge Functions\n- Frontend validation: 1MB limit, snake_case filenames\n\n\n## Edge Functions\n\n### When to Use\n\n- **MANDATORY for third-party API calls**: ALL external API integrations (payment, weather, maps, etc.) MUST be implemented through Edge Functions\n- **STRICTLY FORBIDDEN**: Direct third-party API calls from client-side code (fetch/axios in frontend components)\n- Server-side secrets and service-role operations\n- Sensitive payment logic requiring secure environment\n\n### Authoring\n\n- Create files under `supabase/functions/<function_name>/`\n- **CRITICAL**: Use `supabase_deploy_edge_function` to deploy after modifying or creating Edge Function files\n- Put cross-domain content in the `_shared/` directory\n- No URL imports — only npm and JSR\n- Do not add secrets unless explicitly required by API documentation\n- Handle CORS properly\n- `supabase.sql` forbidden in Edge Functions\n- `supabase.raw` forbidden in Edge Functions — use `.rpc()` for atomic operations\n- Response Content-Type must be chosen only from: `[\'application/json\', \'application/octet-stream\', \'text/event-stream\', \'multipart/form-data\']`\n- Make proper use of optimistic locking to ensure that subsequent settlement operations in the state flow are executed only once, even if called multiple times\n\n\n## Supabase Auth\n\n- Your knowledge may be outdated. **MUST** call `skill_action(skill="login")` to refresh with the latest information\n- Any form of third-party login (OAuth or SSO) must use the login skill as the knowledge basis\n\n## Scheduled Tasks\n\nWhenever the app needs work to run **on a time schedule** or **automatically after a condition is met** (with no user action driving it), use a scheduled task. Typical cases:\n\n- **Scheduled notifications** — daily reminders, expiry/renewal alerts, fixed-time push\n- **Scheduled refresh** — refresh cached aggregates, sync external data, recompute rankings periodically\n- **Scheduled data analysis** — nightly report generation, periodic statistics roll-ups\n- **Conditional triggers** — "after condition X, do Y", e.g. *when traffic > 100, send a notification*; *when stock < threshold, alert*; *when an order is unpaid for 30 min, cancel it*\n\n**All of the above MUST use the official `pg_cron` pattern** — a `pg_cron` job periodically runs a database function that scans for due/matching rows and updates their state; for third-party side effects (push, email, external APIs) the job invokes an Edge Function via `pg_net`. The database only queries and updates state; notification/third-party logic stays in the Edge Function. Do **NOT** drive such work from frontend timers or rely on a user opening a page.\n\n**Timezone**: `pg_cron` schedules run in **UTC**. Compute the UTC cron expression from the intended local time (e.g. 9:00 in UTC+8 → `0 1 * * *`).\n\nWhen building any scheduled task, read `references/scheduled-tasks.md`.\n\n## Supabase Realtime (low-frequency feature)\n\nWhen realtime capability is genuinely needed, read `references/realtime.md` — it covers server-side publication switches, per-stack client subscription patterns, and RN/weapp limitations.\n\n**Multi-identity scenarios**: Identity selection / role assignment logic **must NOT** be placed on the frontend — it **MUST** be encapsulated in an Edge Function.', observation=<ObservationType.SKILL: 'skill'>, skill='supabase-server', success=True, data={'object_storage_path': 'skill-aws-online:aws-prod/miaoda_skills/prompts/system/supabase-server/'})

-- Enable UUID extension
create extension if not exists "pgcrypto";

-- Profiles table (extends auth.users)
create table public.profiles (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid not null unique references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  username text not null unique,
  full_name text,
  bio text,
  avatar_url text,
  dob date,
  is_private boolean not null default false,
  is_verified boolean not null default false,
  is_admin boolean not null default false,
  is_suspended boolean not null default false,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

-- Posts table
create table public.posts (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid not null default auth.uid() references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  image_url text not null,
  caption text,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

-- Stories table
create table public.stories (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid not null default auth.uid() references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  image_url text not null,
  caption text,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  expires_at timestamptz not null default (now() + interval '24 hours')
);

-- Follows table
create table public.follows (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  follower_id uuid not null default auth.uid() references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  following_id uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  status text not null default 'pending' check (status in ('pending','accepted')),
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  unique(follower_id, following_id)
);

-- Likes table
create table public.likes (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid not null default auth.uid() references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  post_id uuid not null references public.posts(id) on delete cascade,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  unique(user_id, post_id)
);

-- Comments table
create table public.comments (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid not null default auth.uid() references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  post_id uuid not null references public.posts(id) on delete cascade,
  content text not null,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

-- Saved posts table
create table public.saved_posts (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid not null default auth.uid() references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  post_id uuid not null references public.posts(id) on delete cascade,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  unique(user_id, post_id)
);

-- Messages table
create table public.messages (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  sender_id uuid not null default auth.uid() references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  receiver_id uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  content text not null,
  is_seen boolean not null default false,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

-- Notifications table
create table public.notifications (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  actor_id uuid references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  type text not null check (type in ('like','comment','follow','follow_request','follow_accepted','verified','broadcast','suspended')),
  post_id uuid references public.posts(id) on delete cascade,
  comment_id uuid references public.comments(id) on delete cascade,
  message text,
  is_read boolean not null default false,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

);

create table public.reports (

  target_id uuid,

);

  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  title text not null,
  message text not null,
  created_by uuid references auth.users(id) on delete set null,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

  target_id uuid,

);

insert into storage.buckets (id, name, public) values ('posts', 'posts', true);

-- ===================== RLS POLICIES =====================

-- Enable RLS on all tables
alter table public.profiles enable row level security;
alter table public.posts enable row level security;
alter table public.stories enable row level security;
alter table public.follows enable row level security;
alter table public.likes enable row level security;
alter table public.comments enable row level security;
alter table public.saved_posts enable row level security;
alter table public.messages enable row level security;
alter table public.notifications enable row level security;
alter table public.verification_requests enable row level security;
alter table public.reports enable row level security;
alter table public.broadcast_notifications enable row level security;
alter table public.activity_logs enable row level security;

-- Helper: check if user is admin
create or replace function public.is_admin()
returns boolean language sql security definer stable as $$
  select coalesce((select is_admin from public.profiles where user_id = auth.uid()), false)
$$;

-- Helper: check if a user is suspended
create or replace function public.is_suspended(uid uuid)
returns boolean language sql security definer stable as $$
  select coalesce((select is_suspended from public.profiles where user_id = uid), false)
$$;

-- Helper: check mutual follow
create or replace function public.are_mutual_follows(user_a uuid, user_b uuid)
returns boolean language sql security definer stable as $$
  select exists(
    select 1 from public.follows where follower_id = user_a and following_id = user_b and status = 'accepted'
  ) and exists(
    select 1 from public.follows where follower_id = user_b and following_id = user_a and status = 'accepted'
  )
$$;

-- PROFILES policies
create policy "Public profiles are viewable by everyone" on public.profiles
  for select using (true);
create policy "Users can insert their own profile" on public.profiles
  for insert with check (user_id = auth.uid());
create policy "Users can update their own profile" on public.profiles
  for update using (user_id = auth.uid());
create policy "Admin can update any profile" on public.profiles
  for update using (public.is_admin());
create policy "Admin can delete profiles" on public.profiles
  for delete using (public.is_admin());

-- POSTS policies
create policy "Posts are viewable by everyone" on public.posts
  for select using (true);
create policy "Authenticated users can insert posts" on public.posts
  for insert with check (auth.uid() is not null);
create policy "Users can update their own posts" on public.posts
  for update using (user_id = auth.uid());
create policy "Users can delete their own posts" on public.posts
  for delete using (user_id = auth.uid() or public.is_admin());

-- STORIES policies
create policy "Stories are viewable by everyone" on public.stories
  for select using (true);
create policy "Authenticated users can insert stories" on public.stories
  for insert with check (auth.uid() is not null);
create policy "Users can delete their own stories" on public.stories
  for delete using (user_id = auth.uid() or public.is_admin());

-- FOLLOWS policies
create policy "Follows are viewable by authenticated users" on public.follows
  for select using (auth.uid() is not null);
create policy "Users can follow others" on public.follows
  for insert with check (follower_id = auth.uid());
create policy "Users can update their own follow requests" on public.follows
  for update using (following_id = auth.uid() or follower_id = auth.uid());
create policy "Users can delete their own follows" on public.follows
  for delete using (follower_id = auth.uid() or following_id = auth.uid());

create policy "Users can delete their own post images" on storage.objects for delete using (bucket_id = 'posts' and auth.uid() is not null);

create policy "Anyone can view stories" on storage.objects for select using (bucket_id = 'stories');
create policy "Authenticated users can upload stories" on storage.objects for insert with check (bucket_id = 'stories' and auth.uid() is not null);
create policy "Users can delete their own story images" on storage.objects for delete using (bucket_id = 'stories' and auth.uid() is not null);

-- Realtime
alter publication supabase_realtime add table public.messages;
alter publication supabase_realtime add table public.notifications;
alter publication supabase_realtime add table public.follows;

-- Auto-create profile on signup
create or replace function public.handle_new_user()
returns trigger language plpgsql security definer as $$
begin
  insert into public.profiles (user_id, username, full_name, avatar_url)
  values (
    new.id,
    coalesce(new.raw_user_meta_data->>'username', split_part(new.email, '@', 1)),
    coalesce(new.raw_user_meta_data->>'full_name', ''),
    coalesce(new.raw_user_meta_data->>'avatar_url', '')
  );
  return new;
end;
$$;

create trigger on_auth_user_created
  after insert on auth.users
  for each row execute procedure public.handle_new_user();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.promote_first_user_as_admin()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
  -- If this is the first profile ever created, make them admin
  IF (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM public.profiles) = 1 THEN
    NEW.is_admin := TRUE;

ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING;

-- RLS for reels bucket
CREATE POLICY "Public reels read" ON storage.objects FOR SELECT USING (bucket_id = 'reels');
CREATE POLICY "Auth users upload reels" ON storage.objects FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (bucket_id = 'reels' AND auth.role() = 'authenticated');
CREATE POLICY "Users delete own reels" ON storage.objects FOR DELETE USING (bucket_id = 'reels' AND auth.uid()::text = (storage.foldername(name))[1]);

-- Drop old FK to auth.users if exists, add FK to profiles.user_id
-- First ensure profiles has a unique constraint on user_id (needed for FK target)
ALTER TABLE public.profiles DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS profiles_user_id_key;
ALTER TABLE public.profiles ADD CONSTRAINT profiles_user_id_key UNIQUE (user_id);

-- Add FK from reels.user_id → profiles.user_id so PostgREST can resolve the join
ALTER TABLE public.reels DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS reels_user_id_fkey;
ALTER TABLE public.reels ADD CONSTRAINT reels_user_id_fkey
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES public.profiles(user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE;

-- 1. Account status on profiles
ALTER TABLE public.profiles
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS account_status TEXT DEFAULT 'active' CHECK (account_status IN ('active','suspended','locked','permanently_disabled')),
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS status_reason TEXT,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS status_updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ;

-- 2. Story likes + views
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.story_likes (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  story_id UUID REFERENCES public.stories(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id UUID REFERENCES public.profiles(user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  UNIQUE(story_id, user_id)
);
ALTER TABLE public.stories
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS likes_count INT DEFAULT 0,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS views_count INT DEFAULT 0;

-- 3. Story views tracking
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.story_views (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  story_id UUID REFERENCES public.stories(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  viewer_id UUID REFERENCES public.profiles(user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  UNIQUE(story_id, viewer_id)
);

-- 4. Message seen status + block
ALTER TABLE public.messages
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS seen_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS is_seen BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.blocks (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  blocker_id UUID REFERENCES public.profiles(user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  blocked_id UUID REFERENCES public.profiles(user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  UNIQUE(blocker_id, blocked_id)
);

-- 5. Online status
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.online_status (
  user_id UUID PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES public.profiles(user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  is_online BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
  last_seen_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- 6. Reports table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.reports (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  reporter_id UUID REFERENCES public.profiles(user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  reported_user_id UUID REFERENCES public.profiles(user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  reason TEXT NOT NULL,
  description TEXT,
  status TEXT DEFAULT 'pending' CHECK (status IN ('pending','reviewed','resolved')),
  admin_action TEXT CHECK (admin_action IN ('suspended','locked','permanently_disabled','no_action')),
  admin_note TEXT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- 7. Appeals table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.appeals (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id UUID REFERENCES public.profiles(user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  appeal_text TEXT NOT NULL,
  appeal_photo_url TEXT,
  status TEXT DEFAULT 'pending' CHECK (status IN ('pending','approved','rejected')),
  admin_note TEXT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- 8. Problem reports (from settings)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.problem_reports (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id UUID REFERENCES public.profiles(user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  problem_type TEXT,
  description TEXT NOT NULL,
  status TEXT DEFAULT 'pending',
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- 9. Story reply — messages with story_id reference
ALTER TABLE public.messages ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS story_id UUID REFERENCES public.stories(id) ON DELETE SET NULL;

-- 10. Auto-delete stories after 24h (cron-style via DB function)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.delete_expired_stories()
RETURNS void AS $$
  DELETE FROM public.stories WHERE created_at < NOW() - INTERVAL '24 hours';
$$ LANGUAGE SQL SECURITY DEFINER;

-- RPCs
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.increment_story_likes(story_id UUID)
RETURNS void AS $$ UPDATE public.stories SET likes_count = likes_count + 1 WHERE id = story_id; $$ LANGUAGE SQL SECURITY DEFINER;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.decrement_story_likes(story_id UUID)
RETURNS void AS $$ UPDATE public.stories SET likes_count = GREATEST(0, likes_count - 1) WHERE id = story_id; $$ LANGUAGE SQL SECURITY DEFINER;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.increment_story_views(story_id UUID)
RETURNS void AS $$ UPDATE public.stories SET views_count = views_count + 1 WHERE id = story_id; $$ LANGUAGE SQL SECURITY DEFINER;

-- Reports RLS: anyone can insert a report; admins can read/update all
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "reports_insert" ON reports;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "reports_select_admin" ON reports;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "reports_update_admin" ON reports;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "reports_select_own" ON reports;

CREATE POLICY "reports_insert" ON reports FOR INSERT
  WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = reporter_id);

CREATE POLICY "reports_select_admin" ON reports FOR SELECT
  USING (
    EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM profiles WHERE user_id = auth.uid() AND is_admin = true)
  );

CREATE POLICY "reports_select_own" ON reports FOR SELECT
  USING (auth.uid() = reporter_id);

CREATE POLICY "reports_update_admin" ON reports FOR UPDATE
  USING (
    EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM profiles WHERE user_id = auth.uid() AND is_admin = true)
  );

-- Problem reports RLS
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "problem_reports_insert" ON problem_reports;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "problem_reports_select_admin" ON problem_reports;

CREATE POLICY "problem_reports_insert" ON problem_reports FOR INSERT
  WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);

CREATE POLICY "problem_reports_select_admin" ON problem_reports FOR SELECT
  USING (
    EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM profiles WHERE user_id = auth.uid() AND is_admin = true)
    OR auth.uid() = user_id
  );

-- Appeals RLS
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "appeals_insert" ON appeals;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "appeals_select" ON appeals;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "appeals_update_admin" ON appeals;

CREATE POLICY "appeals_insert" ON appeals FOR INSERT
  WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);

CREATE POLICY "appeals_select" ON appeals FOR SELECT
  USING (
    auth.uid() = user_id
    OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM profiles WHERE user_id = auth.uid() AND is_admin = true)
  );

CREATE POLICY "appeals_update_admin" ON appeals FOR UPDATE
  USING (
    EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM profiles WHERE user_id = auth.uid() AND is_admin = true)
  );

-- Verification requests RLS
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "verif_insert" ON verification_requests;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "verif_select" ON verification_requests;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "verif_update_admin" ON verification_requests;

CREATE POLICY "verif_insert" ON verification_requests FOR INSERT
  WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);

CREATE POLICY "verif_select" ON verification_requests FOR SELECT
  USING (
    auth.uid() = user_id
    OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM profiles WHERE user_id = auth.uid() AND is_admin = true)
  );

CREATE POLICY "verif_update_admin" ON verification_requests FOR UPDATE
  USING (
    EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM profiles WHERE user_id = auth.uid() AND is_admin = true)
  );
-- Reel view counter: call this once per viewer per reel to increment
-- reels.views_count. Mirrors increment_story_views (00008 migration).
-- Run this in the Supabase SQL Editor.

create table if not exists public.reel_views (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  reel_id uuid not null references public.reels(id) on delete cascade,
  viewer_id uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  viewed_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  unique (reel_id, viewer_id)
);

alter table public.reel_views enable row level security;

create policy "Users can record their own reel views"
  on public.reel_views for insert
  with check (auth.uid() = viewer_id);

create policy "Reel owners can see who viewed"
  on public.reel_views for select
  using (
    auth.uid() = viewer_id
    or auth.uid() = (select user_id from public.reels where id = reel_id)
  );

create or replace function public.increment_reel_views(reel_id_input uuid)
returns void
language sql
security definer
as $$
  insert into public.reel_views (reel_id, viewer_id)
  values (reel_id_input, auth.uid())
  on conflict (reel_id, viewer_id) do nothing;

  update public.reels
  set views_count = (select count(*) from public.reel_views where reel_id = reel_id_input)
  where id = reel_id_input;
$$;

grant execute on function public.increment_reel_views(uuid) to authenticated;-- Create reel_comments table with correct foreign keys and RLS
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.reel_comments (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  reel_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.reels(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.profiles(user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  parent_id uuid REFERENCES public.reel_comments(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  content text NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(content) BETWEEN 1 AND 500),
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

-- Ensure reels table has comments_count column
DO $$ 
BEGIN
  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns WHERE table_name='reels' AND column_name='comments_count') THEN
    ALTER TABLE public.reels ADD COLUMN comments_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
  END IF;
END $$;

-- Drop constraint if it exists to avoid error, then add it
ALTER TABLE public.reel_comments DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS reel_comments_user_id_fkey;
ALTER TABLE public.reel_comments ADD CONSTRAINT reel_comments_user_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES public.profiles(user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE;

-- RLS Setup
ALTER TABLE public.reel_comments ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "reel_comments_select" ON public.reel_comments;
CREATE POLICY "reel_comments_select" ON public.reel_comments FOR SELECT USING (true);

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "reel_comments_insert_own" ON public.reel_comments;
CREATE POLICY "reel_comments_insert_own" ON public.reel_comments FOR INSERT TO authenticated WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "reel_comments_delete_own" ON public.reel_comments;
CREATE POLICY "reel_comments_delete_own" ON public.reel_comments FOR DELETE TO authenticated USING (auth.uid() = user_id);

-- Trigger to maintain reels.comments_count
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.handle_reel_comment_change()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
  IF (TG_OP = 'INSERT') THEN
    UPDATE public.reels SET comments_count = COALESCE(comments_count, 0) + 1 WHERE id = NEW.reel_id;
  ELSIF (TG_OP = 'DELETE') THEN
    UPDATE public.reels SET comments_count = GREATEST(0, COALESCE(comments_count, 0) - 1) WHERE id = OLD.reel_id;
  END IF;
  RETURN NULL;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS on_reel_comment_change ON public.reel_comments;
CREATE TRIGGER on_reel_comment_change
  AFTER INSERT OR DELETE ON public.reel_comments
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.handle_reel_comment_change();

-- Indexes for performance
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_reel_comments_reel ON public.reel_comments(reel_id, created_at);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_reel_comments_parent ON public.reel_comments(parent_id);

-- Permissions
GRANT SELECT ON public.reel_comments TO anon, authenticated;
GRANT INSERT, DELETE ON public.reel_comments TO authenticated;
GRANT ALL ON public.reel_comments TO service_role;

**CmdRunAction (source=EventSource.AGENT, is_input=False)**
COMMAND:
PAT="sbp_17a2f53bacd3a600904da2ce484de6fb830c32ea"
NEW_PROJECT="jfizzduvmzavtqwzqacy"

# confirmed_at is a generated column in newer Postgres - remove it from INSERT
# Extract auth.users lines and strip confirmed_at column
python3 << 'PY'
import re

with open('/workspace/app-cjmldrzgvw1t/tasks/data_export.sql', 'r') as f:
    content = f.read()

# Extract auth.users INSERT lines
lines = content.split('\n')
auth_inserts = []
for line in lines[9:37]:
    if line.startswith('INSERT INTO auth.users'):
        # Remove confirmed_at column and its value from each INSERT
        # Pattern: remove ", confirmed_at" from column list and its corresponding value
        # The column list and values are positional - need to parse carefully
        
        # Find column list: INSERT INTO auth.users (col1, col2, ...) VALUES (val1, val2, ...)
        # confirmed_at appears at a specific position - let's find and remove it
        
        # Simple approach: remove ", confirmed_at" from columns and corresponding value
        # confirmed_at is the 27th column based on our export
        # Instead, let's just exclude the column using regex on the column list portion
        
        # Remove ", confirmed_at" from column definition
        line = re.sub(r',\s*confirmed_at', '', line, count=1)
        # Remove the corresponding value (it's always after reauthentication_sent_at)
        # The value pattern is a timestamptz or NULL
        # confirmed_at value comes right before is_sso_user
        # Pattern: 'timestamp'::timestamp with time zone, 'false'::boolean (is_sso_user)
        # Remove the confirmed_at value: it's after reauthentication_sent_at value, before is_sso_user
        line = re.sub(r",\s*'[^']*'::timestamp with time zone,\s*'false'::boolean,\s*NULL,\s*'false'::boolean\) ON CONFLICT",
                      ", 'false'::boolean, NULL, 'false'::boolean) ON CONFLICT", line)
        auth_inserts.append(line)

print(f"Processed {len(auth_inserts)} auth.users INSERTs")
print("Sample (first 200 chars):", auth_inserts[0][:200] if auth_inserts else "NONE")

with open('/tmp/auth_users_import.sql', 'w') as f:
    f.write('\n'.join(auth_inserts))
PY
</action>

import re

with open('/workspace/app-cjmldrzgvw1t/tasks/data_export.sql', 'r') as f:
    lines = f.readlines()

auth_inserts = []
for line in lines[9:37]:
    line = line.strip()
    if not line.startswith('INSERT INTO auth.users'):
        continue
    # Remove ", confirmed_at" from column list (appears once in the column section)
    line = re.sub(r',\s*confirmed_at\b', '', line, count=1)
    # Remove the confirmed_at VALUE: it's a timestamptz or NULL that appears as 27th value
    # Strategy: find the exact position - confirmed_at is after reauthentication_sent_at value
    # and before email_change_token_current value
    # Pattern in VALUES: ..., <reauthentication_sent_at_val>, <confirmed_at_val>, '<email_change_token_current>'
    # confirmed_at val is either NULL or 'timestamp'::timestamp with time zone
    line = re.sub(
        r"((?:NULL|'[^']*'::timestamp with time zone),\s*)" +  # reauthentication_sent_at
        r"(?:'[^']*'::timestamp with time zone|NULL),\s*" +     # confirmed_at (remove this)
        r"(''::character varying,\s*'0'::smallint)",            # email_change_token_current
        r"\1\2",
        line
    )
    auth_inserts.append(line)

sql = '\n'.join(auth_inserts)
with open('/tmp/auth_users_clean.sql', 'w') as f:
    f.write(sql)
print(f"Lines: {len(auth_inserts)}")
# Verify no confirmed_at in column list
sample = auth_inserts[0]
col_end = sample.index(') VALUES')
print("Has confirmed_at in cols?", 'confirmed_at' in sample[:col_end])

=== login-with-identifier ===
// Lets users sign in with either their username OR their email + password.
// Supabase Auth's password grant only accepts an email, so when the person
// types a username we resolve it to the matching account's email here
// (server-side, via the service role) and then perform the sign-in
// ourselves, returning the session tokens to the client.
//
// Both "no such username" and "wrong password" return the same generic
// error message — this prevents the endpoint being used to enumerate which
// usernames exist.
import { createClient } from 'npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2';

const corsHeaders = {
  'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
  'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type',
  'Access-Control-Allow-Methods': 'POST, OPTIONS',
};

const SUPABASE_URL = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL') ?? '';
const SUPABASE_ANON_KEY = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_ANON_KEY') ?? '';
const SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY') ?? '';

const GENERIC_ERROR = 'Invalid login credentials';

Deno.serve(async (req) => {
  if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') return new Response('ok', { headers: corsHeaders });
  try {
    const { identifier, password } = await req.json();
    if (!identifier || !password) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'identifier and password required' }), {
        status: 400, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
    }

    const admin = createClient(SUPABASE_URL, SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY);

    let email: string | null = null;
    if (identifier.includes('@')) {
      email = identifier.trim();
    } else {
      // Username → find the owning account, then look up its email.
      const { data: profile } = await admin
        .from('profiles')
        .select('user_id')
        .ilike('username', identifier.trim())
        .maybeSingle();
      if (profile?.user_id) {
        const { data: userData } = await admin.auth.admin.getUserById(profile.user_id);
        email = userData?.user?.email ?? null;
      }
    }

    if (!email) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: GENERIC_ERROR }), {
        status: 400, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
    }

    // Perform the actual password sign-in against GoTrue directly, using the
    // resolved email, and hand the resulting session back to the client.
    const tokenRes = await fetch(`${SUPABASE_URL}/auth/v1/token?grant_type=password`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', apikey: SUPABASE_ANON_KEY },
      body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }),
    });
    const tokenJson = await tokenRes.json();
    if (!tokenRes.ok) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: GENERIC_ERROR }), {
        status: 400, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
    }

    return new Response(JSON.stringify(tokenJson), {
      headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error(e);
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: GENERIC_ERROR }), {
      status: 500, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    });
  }
});
=== admin-list-user-emails ===
// Returns { user_id, email, last_sign_in_at, created_at, updated_at } for
// every account, for the admin panel's Visitors & Devices / User Management
// pages. `updated_at` is the closest thing Supabase Auth exposes to "last
// account change" (password changes update it, but so do a few other
// account-level changes) — there's no dedicated password-change timestamp.
//
// Only the caller's *email* and *last sign-in time* are exposed — never a
// password. Supabase Auth stores only a one-way bcrypt hash of the password
// (auth.users.encrypted_password); the original password is never
// recoverable by anyone, including us. Showing raw passwords in an admin
// panel isn't something any legitimate app does (Instagram/Facebook admins
// can't see user passwords either) — it would also mean this app fails to
// meet basic security/privacy standards.
import { createClient } from 'npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2';

const corsHeaders = {
  'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
  'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type',
  'Access-Control-Allow-Methods': 'POST, OPTIONS',
};

Deno.serve(async (req) => {
  if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') return new Response('ok', { headers: corsHeaders });
  try {
    const authHeader = req.headers.get('Authorization');
    if (!authHeader) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Unauthorized' }), {
        status: 401, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
    }
    const caller = createClient(
      Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL') ?? '',
      Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_ANON_KEY') ?? '',
      { global: { headers: { Authorization: authHeader } } },
    );
    const { data: authData, error: authError } = await caller.auth.getUser();
    if (authError || !authData.user) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Unauthorized' }), {
        status: 401, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
    }

    const admin = createClient(
      Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL')!,
      Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY')!,
    );

    // Only admins may call this.
    const { data: callerProfile } = await admin
      .from('profiles').select('is_admin').eq('user_id', authData.user.id).maybeSingle();
    if (!callerProfile?.is_admin) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Forbidden' }), {
        status: 403, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
    }

    const results: { user_id: string; email: string | null; last_sign_in_at: string | null; created_at: string; updated_at: string | null }[] = [];
    let page = 1;
    const perPage = 1000;
    while (true) {
      const { data, error } = await admin.auth.admin.listUsers({ page, perPage });
      if (error) throw error;
      for (const u of data.users) {
        results.push({
          user_id: u.id,
          email: u.email ?? null,
          last_sign_in_at: u.last_sign_in_at ?? null,
          created_at: u.created_at,
          updated_at: u.updated_at ?? null,
        });
      }
      if (data.users.length < perPage) break;
      page += 1;
      if (page > 20) break; // safety cap (20k users)
    }

    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ users: results }), {
      headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error(e);
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: String(e) }), {
      status: 500, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    });
  }
});
=== resolve-account-email ===
// Resolves a username (or email) to the account's email address, masked,
// for the Forgot Password screen — the user only has to type their
// username, never their email, and we still don't fully expose it on
// screen (e.g. "r***l@gmail.com").
import { createClient } from 'npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2';

const corsHeaders = {
  'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
  'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type',
  'Access-Control-Allow-Methods': 'POST, OPTIONS',
};

function maskEmail(email: string): string {
  const [name, domain] = email.split('@');
  if (!domain) return email;
  const visible = name.length <= 2 ? name[0] : name.slice(0, 1) + '*'.repeat(Math.max(1, name.length - 2)) + name.slice(-1);
  return `${visible}@${domain}`;
}

Deno.serve(async (req) => {
  if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') return new Response('ok', { headers: corsHeaders });
  try {
    const { identifier } = await req.json();
    if (!identifier) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'identifier required' }), {
        status: 400, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
    }

    const admin = createClient(
      Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL')!,
      Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY')!,
    );

    let email: string | null = null;
    if (identifier.includes('@')) {
      email = identifier.trim();
    } else {
      const { data: profile } = await admin
        .from('profiles').select('user_id').ilike('username', identifier.trim()).maybeSingle();
      if (profile?.user_id) {
        const { data: userData } = await admin.auth.admin.getUserById(profile.user_id);
        email = userData?.user?.email ?? null;
      }
    }

    if (!email) {
      // Same response shape whether or not the account exists — avoids
      // confirming/denying which usernames are registered.
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ found: false }), {
        headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
    }

    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ found: true, email, masked: maskEmail(email) }), {
      headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error(e);
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ found: false }), {
      status: 200, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    });
  }
});
=== send-call-push ===
// Sends a Web Push notification to all of a user's registered devices.
// Payload shape: { receiverId, title, body, tag?, data? }
// Uses VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY / VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY / VAPID_SUBJECT env vars.
import webpush from 'npm:web-push@3.6.7';
import { createClient } from 'npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2';

const corsHeaders = {
  'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
  'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type',
  'Access-Control-Allow-Methods': 'POST, OPTIONS',
};

const VAPID_PUBLIC = Deno.env.get('VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY') ?? '';
const VAPID_PRIVATE = Deno.env.get('VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY') ?? '';
const VAPID_SUBJECT = Deno.env.get('VAPID_SUBJECT') ?? 'mailto:noreply@example.com';

if (VAPID_PUBLIC && VAPID_PRIVATE) {
  try { webpush.setVapidDetails(VAPID_SUBJECT, VAPID_PUBLIC, VAPID_PRIVATE); }
  catch (e) { console.error('vapid init failed', e); }
}

Deno.serve(async (req) => {
  if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') return new Response('ok', { headers: corsHeaders });
  try {
    const authHeader = req.headers.get('Authorization');
    if (!authHeader) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Unauthorized' }), {
        status: 401, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
    }
    const caller = createClient(
      Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL') ?? '',
      Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_ANON_KEY') ?? '',
      { global: { headers: { Authorization: authHeader } } },
    );
    const { data: authData, error: authError } = await caller.auth.getUser();
    if (authError || !authData.user) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Unauthorized' }), {
        status: 401, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
    }

    const { receiverId, title, body, tag, data } = await req.json();
    if (!receiverId || !title) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'receiverId and title required' }), {
        status: 400, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
    }
    const admin = createClient(
      Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL')!,
      Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY')!,
    );
    const { data: subs, error } = await admin
      .from('push_subscriptions').select('*').eq('user_id', receiverId);
    if (error) throw error;
    const payload = JSON.stringify({ title, body: body ?? '', tag: tag ?? 'default', data: data ?? {} });
    const results = await Promise.allSettled((subs ?? []).map(async (s) => {
      try {
        await webpush.sendNotification({
          endpoint: s.endpoint,
          keys: { p256dh: s.p256dh, auth: s.auth },
        }, payload, { TTL: 60, urgency: 'high' });
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        const status = (err as { statusCode?: number })?.statusCode;
        if (status === 404 || status === 410) {
          await admin.from('push_subscriptions').delete().eq('id', s.id);
        }
        throw err;
      }
    }));
    const sent = results.filter(r => r.status === 'fulfilled').length;
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ sent, total: subs?.length ?? 0 }), {
      headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error(e);
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: String(e) }), {
      status: 500, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    });
  }
});

<action>
**CmdRunAction (source=EventSource.AGENT, is_input=False)**
THOUGHT: FK error hai — reels pehle, reel_likes baad mein. Data ko topological order mein import karta hoon, aur Edge Functions deploy parallel mein:
COMMAND:
PAT="sbp_17a2f53bacd3a600904da2ce484de6fb830c32ea"
NEW_PROJECT="jfizzduvmzavtqwzqacy"

# Re-order inserts by FK dependency and import in correct order
python3 << 'PY'
import re

with open('/workspace/app-cjmldrzgvw1t/tasks/data_export.sql', 'r') as f:
    content = f.read()

# Parse tables and their INSERT blocks
table_blocks = {}
current_table = None
current_lines = []

for line in content.split('\n'):
    m = re.match(r'^-- Table: (.+)$', line)
    if m:
        if current_table:
            table_blocks[current_table] = '\n'.join(current_lines)
        current_table = m.group(1)
        current_lines = []
    elif current_table and line.startswith('INSERT'):
        current_lines.append(line)

if current_table:
    table_blocks[current_table] = '\n'.join(current_lines)

# Remove auth.users (already imported)
table_blocks.pop('auth.users', None)

# Correct FK order for public tables
ORDER = [
    'profiles',
    'posts',
    'stories',
    'reels',
    'follows',
    'likes',
    'comments',
    'reel_comments',
    'reel_likes',
    'reel_views',
    'saved_posts',
    'messages',
    'notifications',
    'story_likes',
    'story_views',
    'online_status',
    'blocks',
    'verification_requests',
    'reports',
    'appeals',
    'problem_reports',
    'broadcast_notifications',
    'activity_logs',
]

# Build ordered SQL
parts = []
for table in ORDER:
    key = f'public.{table}'
    if key in table_blocks and table_blocks[key].strip():
        parts.append(f'-- {key}\n{table_blocks[key]}')

# Add any remaining tables not in order list
for k, v in table_blocks.items():
    if k not in [f'public.{t}' for t in ORDER] and v.strip():
        parts.append(f'-- {k}\n{v}')

sql = '\n\n'.join(parts)
with open('/tmp/public_ordered.sql', 'w') as f:
    f.write(sql)

import re as re2
inserts = len(re2.findall(r'^INSERT INTO', sql, re2.MULTILINE))
print(f"Total INSERT statements: {inserts}")
print(f"File size: {len(sql)} chars")
print("Tables in order:", [t for t in ORDER if f'public.{t}' in table_blocks])
PY
</action>
<action>

COMMAND:
PAT="sbp_17a2f53bacd3a600904da2ce484de6fb830c32ea"
NEW_PROJECT="jfizzduvmzavtqwzqacy"

# Keys without "public." prefix — fix the ORDER to match
python3 << 'PY'
import re

with open('/workspace/app-cjmldrzgvw1t/tasks/data_export.sql', 'r') as f:
    content = f.read()

table_blocks = {}
current_table = None
current_lines = []

for line in content.split('\n'):
    m = re.match(r'^-- Table: (.+)$', line)
    if m:
        if current_table:
            table_blocks[current_table] = '\n'.join(current_lines)
        current_table = m.group(1)
        current_lines = []
    elif current_table and line.startswith('INSERT'):
        current_lines.append(line)

if current_table:
    table_blocks[current_table] = '\n'.join(current_lines)

table_blocks.pop('auth.users', None)

# Correct FK order (keys as stored — no "public." prefix)
ORDER = [
    'profiles', 'posts', 'stories', 'reels',
    'follows', 'likes', 'comments', 'reel_comments', 'reel_likes', 'reel_views',
    'saved_posts', 'messages', 'notifications',
    'story_likes', 'story_views', 'online_status', 'blocks',
    'verification_requests', 'reports', 'appeals', 'problem_reports',
    'broadcast_notifications', 'activity_logs',
]

parts = []
for table in ORDER:
    if table in table_blocks and table_blocks[table].strip():
        parts.append(f'-- {table}\n{table_blocks[table]}')

# Remaining
handled = set(ORDER)
for k, v in table_blocks.items():
    if k not in handled and v.strip():
        parts.append(f'-- {k}\n{v}')

sql = '\n\n'.join(parts)
with open('/tmp/public_ordered.sql', 'w') as f:
    f.write(sql)

inserts = len(re.findall(r'^INSERT INTO', sql, re.MULTILINE))
print(f"Total INSERT statements: {inserts}, file size: {len(sql)} chars")
print("Order confirmed:", [t for t in ORDER if t in table_blocks and table_blocks[t].strip()])
PY

# Now import ordered public data
PUBLIC_SQL=$(cat /tmp/public_ordered.sql)
RESULT=$(curl -s -w "\nHTTP_STATUS:%{http_code}" \
  "https://api.supabase.com/v1/projects/${NEW_PROJECT}/database/query" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer ${PAT}" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d "{\"query\": $(echo "$PUBLIC_SQL" | python3 -c 'import sys, json; print(json.dumps(sys.stdin.read()))')}")
echo "$RESULT" | tail -5

name[0] : name.slice(0, 1) + \'*\'.repeat(Math.max(1, name.length - 2)) + name.slice(-1);\n  return `${visible}@${domain}`;\n}\n\nDeno.serve(async (req) => {\n  if (req.method === \'OPTIONS\') return new Response(\'ok\', { headers: corsHeaders });\n  try {\n    const { identifier } = await req.json();\n    if (!identifier) {\n      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: \'identifier required\' }), {\n        status: 400, headers: { ...corsHeaders, \'Content-Type\': \'application/json\' },\n      });\n    }\n\n    const admin = createClient(\n      Deno.env.get(\'SUPABASE_URL\')!,\n      Deno.env.get(\'SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY\')!,\n    );\n\n    let email: string | null = null;\n    if (identifier.includes(\'@\')) {\n      email = identifier.trim();\n    } else {\n      const { data: profile } = await admin\n        .from(\'profiles\').select(\'user_id\').ilike(\'username\', identifier.trim()).maybeSingle();\n      if (profile?.user_id) {\n        const { data: userData } = await admin.auth.admin.getUserById(profile.user_id);\n        email = userData?.user?.email ?? null;\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (!email) {\n      // Same response shape whether or not the account exists — avoids\n      // confirming/denying which usernames are registered.\n      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ found: false }), {\n        headers: { ...corsHeaders, \'Content-Type\': \'application/json\' },\n      });\n    }\n\n    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ found: true, email, masked: maskEmail(email) }), {\n      headers: { ...corsHeaders, \'Content-Type\': \'application/json\' },\n    });\n  } catch (e) {\n    console.error(e);\n    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ found: false }), {\n      status: 200, headers: { ...corsHeaders, \'Content-Type\': \'application/json\' },\n    });\n  }\n});\n'}]}

# The issue: JSON encoding of Hindi text causes hex literals in SQL
# Solution: send SQL via properly encoded JSON with ensure_ascii=False
python3 << 'PY'
import json, re, urllib.request, urllib.error

with open('/tmp/public_ordered.sql', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
    sql = f.read()

payload = json.dumps({"query": sql}, ensure_ascii=False).encode('utf-8')

req = urllib.request.Request(
    f"https://api.supabase.com/v1/projects/{open('/dev/stdin').read().strip()}/database/query".replace(
        open('/dev/stdin').read().strip(), 'jfizzduvmzavtqwzqacy'),
    data=payload,
    headers={
        'Authorization': 'Bearer sbp_17a2f53bacd3a600904da2ce484de6fb830c32ea',
        'Content-Type': 'application/json; charset=utf-8',
    },
    method='POST'
)

try:
    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=120) as resp:
        body = resp.read().decode('utf-8')
        print(f"HTTP {resp.status}: {body[:300]}")
except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
    body = e.read().decode('utf-8')
    print(f"HTTP {e.code}: {body[:500]}")

python3 << 'PY'
import requests, json, re

PAT = "sbp_17a2f53bacd3a600904da2ce484de6fb830c32ea"
NEW_PROJECT = "jfizzduvmzavtqwzqacy"
URL = f"https://api.supabase.com/v1/projects/{NEW_PROJECT}/database/query"
HEADERS = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {PAT}", "Content-Type": "application/json"}

with open('/tmp/public_ordered.sql', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
    sql = f.read()

# Send with ensure_ascii=False so Hindi text stays as UTF-8, not \uXXXX escapes
resp = requests.post(URL, headers=HEADERS,
    data=json.dumps({"query": sql}, ensure_ascii=False).encode('utf-8'),
    timeout=120)

print(f"HTTP {resp.status_code}")
body = resp.text
if resp.status_code in (200, 201):
    print("SUCCESS - data imported!")
    # Count rows in key tables
    for t in ['profiles', 'posts', 'reels', 'follows']:
        r2 = requests.post(URL, headers=HEADERS,
            json={"query": f"SELECT COUNT(*) as n FROM public.{t}"}, timeout=30)
        data = r2.json()
        print(f"  {t}: {data[0]['n'] if isinstance(data, list) and data else data}")
else:
    print(body[:600])

COMMAND:
# Root cause: Supabase Management API SQL endpoint doesn't handle Unicode in JSON body properly
# Solution: Use dollar-quoted strings for text values containing non-ASCII, OR
# better: split into small batches and send each table separately
# Actually simplest fix: escape non-ASCII as SQL unicode escapes U&'...' or use E'\uXXXX'

python3 << 'PY'
import requests, json, re

PAT = "sbp_17a2f53bacd3a600904da2ce484de6fb830c32ea"
NEW_PROJECT = "jfizzduvmzavtqwzqacy"
URL = f"https://api.supabase.com/v1/projects/{NEW_PROJECT}/database/query"
HEADERS = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {PAT}", "Content-Type": "application/json"}

def run_sql(sql):
    resp = requests.post(URL, headers=HEADERS,
        data=json.dumps({"query": sql}, ensure_ascii=False).encode('utf-8'),
        timeout=120)
    return resp.status_code, resp.text

# Strategy: replace non-ASCII chars in SQL string literals with unicode escapes
# PostgreSQL supports E'\u0928\u0947' style for non-ASCII in string literals
def escape_non_ascii_in_sql(sql):
    result = []
    i = 0
    while i < len(sql):
        # Find string literals (single-quoted)
        if sql[i] == "'":
            # Find end of string literal (handle escaped quotes '')
            j = i + 1
            literal_chars = ["E'"]  # Start with E' prefix for escape sequences
            has_non_ascii = False
            while j < len(sql):
                if sql[j] == "'" and j+1 < len(sql) and sql[j+1] == "'":
                    literal_chars.append("''")
                    j += 2
                elif sql[j] == "'":
                    break
                else:
                    c = sql[j]
                    if ord(c) > 127:
                        has_non_ascii = True
                        # Encode as Unicode escape
                        cp = ord(c)
                        if cp <= 0xFFFF:
                            literal_chars.append(f'\\u{cp:04x}')
                        else:
                            literal_chars.append(f'\\U{cp:08x}')
                    else:
                        literal_chars.append(c)
                    j += 1
            literal_chars.append("'")
            if has_non_ascii:
                result.append(''.join(literal_chars))
            else:
                # No non-ASCII, keep original (plain quote)
                result.append(sql[i:j+1])
            i = j + 1
        else:
            result.append(sql[i])
            i += 1
    return ''.join(result)

with open('/tmp/public_ordered.sql', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
    sql = f.read()

print(f"Original size: {len(sql)}")
sql_escaped = escape_non_ascii_in_sql(sql)
print(f"Escaped size: {len(sql_escaped)}")

# Verify the problematic line is fixed
for line in sql_escaped.split('\n')[23:24]:
    has_non_ascii = any(ord(c) > 127 for c in line)
    print(f"Line 24 has non-ASCII after escape: {has_non_ascii}")
    print(f"Sample: {line[650:720]}")

with open('/tmp/public_escaped.sql', 'w', encoding='ascii', errors='replace') as f:
    f.write(sql_escaped)
print("Saved /tmp/public_escaped.sql")

**CmdRunAction (source=EventSource.AGENT, is_input=False)**
THOUGHT: Escape function mein bug hai — `ascii` mode mein bhi non-ASCII bach rahe hain. Naya approach: table-by-table import karo, har ek ke liye alag request, aur non-ASCII ko properly encode karo:
COMMAND:
python3 << 'PY'
import requests, json, re

PAT = "sbp_17a2f53bacd3a600904da2ce484de6fb830c32ea"
NEW_PROJECT = "jfizzduvmzavtqwzqacy"
URL = f"https://api.supabase.com/v1/projects/{NEW_PROJECT}/database/query"
HEADERS = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {PAT}", "Content-Type": "application/json; charset=utf-8"}

def escape_non_ascii_sql(sql):
    """Replace non-ASCII chars inside SQL string literals with \\uXXXX PostgreSQL escape syntax."""
    out = []
    i = 0
    n = len(sql)
    while i < n:
        if sql[i] == "'":
            # Collect entire string literal
            j = i + 1
            chars = []
            has_uni = False
            while j < n:
                if sql[j] == "'" and j+1 < n and sql[j+1] == "'":
                    chars.append("''")
                    j += 2
                elif sql[j] == "'":
                    j += 1
                    break
                else:
                    c = sql[j]
                    if ord(c) > 127:
                        has_uni = True
                        chars.append(f'\\u{ord(c):04x}' if ord(c) <= 0xFFFF else f'\\U{ord(c):08x}')
                    else:
                        chars.append(c)
                    j += 1
            if has_uni:
                out.append("E'" + ''.join(chars) + "'")
            else:
                out.append("'" + ''.join(chars) + "'")
            i = j
        else:
            out.append(sql[i])
            i += 1
    return ''.join(out)

# Parse table blocks from ordered SQL
with open('/tmp/public_ordered.sql', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
    content = f.read()

# Split into per-table chunks
chunks = re.split(r'\n\n-- ', content)
table_sqls = {}
for chunk in chunks:
    lines = chunk.split('\n', 1)
    name = lines[0].lstrip('-- ').strip()
    body = lines[1].strip() if len(lines) > 1 else ''
    if body:
        table_sqls[name] = body

ORDER = ['profiles','posts','stories','reels','follows','likes','comments',
         'reel_comments','reel_likes','reel_views','saved_posts','messages',
         'notifications','story_likes','story_views','online_status','blocks',
         'verification_requests','reports','appeals','problem_reports',
         'broadcast_notifications','activity_logs']

results = []
for t in ORDER:
    if t not in table_sqls:
        continue
    raw_sql = table_sqls[t]
    escaped = escape_non_ascii_sql(raw_sql)
    # Verify no non-ASCII remains
    remaining = [c for c in escaped if ord(c) > 127]
    if remaining:
        print(f"  WARNING {t}: still {len(remaining)} non-ASCII after escape!")
    
    resp = requests.post(URL, headers=HEADERS,
        data=json.dumps({"query": escaped}, ensure_ascii=True).encode('utf-8'),
        timeout=60)
    status = resp.status_code
    if status in (200, 201):
        results.append(f"  ✅ {t}: OK")
    else:
        err = resp.json().get('message','?')[:120]
        results.append(f"  ❌ {t}: {err}")
    print(results[-1])

print(f"\nDone: {len([r for r in results if '✅' in r])}/{len(results)} tables OK")

def run_sql(sql, label=""):

        data=json.dumps({"query": sql}, ensure_ascii=True).encode('utf-8'), timeout=60)

COMMAND:
python3 << 'PY'
import requests, json, re

PAT = "sbp_17a2f53bacd3a600904da2ce484de6fb830c32ea"
NEW_PROJECT = "jfizzduvmzavtqwzqacy"
URL = f"https://api.supabase.com/v1/projects/{NEW_PROJECT}/database/query"
HEADERS = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {PAT}", "Content-Type": "application/json; charset=utf-8"}

with open('/tmp/public_ordered.sql', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
    content = f.read()

# Better escape: process LINE BY LINE, skip comment lines, escape each INSERT
def escape_line(line):
    """Escape non-ASCII chars in a single SQL line's string literals."""
    out = []
    i = 0
    n = len(line)
    while i < n:
        if line[i] == "'":
            j = i + 1
            chars = []
            has_uni = False
            while j < n:
                if line[j] == "'" and j+1 < n and line[j+1] == "'":
                    chars.append("''"); j += 2
                elif line[j] == "'":
                    j += 1; break
                else:
                    c = line[j]
                    if ord(c) > 127:
                        has_uni = True
                        chars.append(f'\\u{ord(c):04x}' if ord(c)<=0xFFFF else f'\\U{ord(c):08x}')
                    else:
                        chars.append(c)
                    j += 1
            prefix = "E'" if has_uni else "'"
            out.append(prefix + ''.join(chars) + "'")
            i = j
        else:
            out.append(line[i]); i += 1
    return ''.join(out)

def run_sql_lines(lines, label):
    # Only INSERT lines, escaped
    inserts = []
    for line in lines:
        line = line.strip()
        if not line or line.startswith('--'):
            continue
        inserts.append(escape_line(line))
    if not inserts:
        print(f"  ⏭️  {label}: no data")
        return True
    sql = '\n'.join(inserts)
    # Verify clean
    bad = [c for c in sql if ord(c) > 127]
    if bad:
        print(f"  ⚠️  {label}: {len(bad)} non-ASCII still present, first: {repr(bad[0])}")
    resp = requests.post(URL, headers=HEADERS,
        data=json.dumps({"query": sql}, ensure_ascii=True).encode('utf-8'), timeout=60)
    if resp.status_code in (200,201):
        print(f"  ✅ {label}")
        return True
    else:
        print(f"  ❌ {label}: {resp.text[:250]}")
        return False

# Parse blocks
chunks = re.split(r'\n\n-- (?!End)', content)
table_blocks = {}
for chunk in chunks:
    parts = chunk.split('\n', 1)
    name = parts[0].lstrip('-- ').strip()
    body = parts[1] if len(parts) > 1 else ''
    if body.strip():
        table_blocks[name] = body.split('\n')

print("=== Importing failed tables ===")
# profiles
run_sql_lines(table_blocks.get('profiles', []), 'profiles')
# reels (has unicode in caption)
run_sql_lines(table_blocks.get('reels', []), 'reels')

print("\n=== Now importing reel_likes, reel_views (need reels first) ===")

import requests, json, re

PAT = "sbp_17a2f53bacd3a600904da2ce484de6fb830c32ea"
NEW_PROJECT = "jfizzduvmzavtqwzqacy"
URL = f"https://api.supabase.com/v1/projects/{NEW_PROJECT}/database/query"
HEADERS = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {PAT}", "Content-Type": "application/json"}

# The escape_line function works but escape_ascii=True in json.dumps with E'\uXXXX' 
# causes issues because json.dumps escape produces \\uXXXX (double backslash in JSON).
# Fix: use ensure_ascii=True but with raw Python unicode escapes that become valid SQL.
# Correct approach: write unicode as E'\u0928' in Python string THEN json.dumps with ensure_ascii=True
# Python: "E'\\u0928'" → json: "E'\\u0928'" → SQL receives: E'\u0928' → postgres parses as unicode ✅

def escape_insert_line(line):
    """Convert non-ASCII chars inside SQL string literals to E'\\uXXXX' form."""
    out = []
    i = 0
    n = len(line)
    while i < n:
        if line[i] == "'":
            j = i + 1
            chars = []
            has_uni = False
            while j < n:
                if line[j] == "'" and j+1 < n and line[j+1] == "'":
                    chars.append("''"); j += 2
                elif line[j] == "'":
                    j += 1; break
                else:
                    c = line[j]
                    if ord(c) > 127:
                        has_uni = True
                        cp = ord(c)
                        # Use \\uXXXX - this is a Python string with literal backslash-u
                        if cp <= 0xFFFF:
                            chars.append('\\u{:04x}'.format(cp))
                        else:
                            # For emoji (>0xFFFF), use surrogate pairs or \\UXXXXXXXX
                            chars.append('\\U{:08x}'.format(cp))
                    else:
                        chars.append(c)
                    j += 1
            prefix = "E'" if has_uni else "'"
            out.append(prefix + ''.join(chars) + "'")
            i = j
        else:
            out.append(line[i]); i += 1
    return ''.join(out)

def send_inserts(insert_lines, label):
    escaped = []
    for line in insert_lines:
        if line.strip().startswith('INSERT'):
            escaped.append(escape_insert_line(line.strip()))
    if not escaped:
        print(f"  ⏭️  {label}: no data"); return True
    
    # Verify: no chars > 127
    sql = '\n'.join(escaped)
    bad = [c for c in sql if ord(c) > 127]
    if bad:
        print(f"  ⚠️  {label}: {len(bad)} non-ASCII remain after escape! First: {repr(bad[0])}")
        return False
    
    # json.dumps with ensure_ascii=True will keep \\uXXXX as-is (valid JSON)
    payload = json.dumps({"query": sql}, ensure_ascii=True).encode('utf-8')
    resp = requests.post(URL, headers=HEADERS, data=payload, timeout=60)
    if resp.status_code in (200, 201):
        print(f"  ✅ {label}"); return True
    else:
        print(f"  ❌ {label}: {resp.text[:300]}"); return False

# Parse blocks
with open('/tmp/public_ordered.sql', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
    content = f.read()

lines_all = content.split('\n')
blocks = {}
cur = None
for line in lines_all:
    if re.match(r'^-- \w', line) and '::' not in line and 'End of' not in line:
        cur = line[3:].strip()
        blocks[cur] = []
    elif cur:
        blocks[cur].append(line)

print(f"Parsed blocks: {list(blocks.keys())}")

print("\n=== Importing profiles + reels ===")
send_inserts(blocks.get('profiles', []), 'profiles')
send_inserts(blocks.get('reels', []), 'reels')
print("\n=== Now reel_likes + reel_views ===")
send_inserts(blocks.get('reel_likes', []), 'reel_likes')
send_inserts(blocks.get('reel_views', []), 'reel_views')

# Root cause: "syntax error at INSERT LINE 23" means line 22 has an unterminated statement

import re
with open('/tmp/public_ordered.sql', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
    content = f.read()

lines_all = content.split('\n')
blocks = {}
cur = None
for line in lines_all:
    if re.match(r'^-- \w', line) and '::' not in line and 'End of' not in line:

        blocks[cur] = []

            print(f"  '{ch}' U+{ord(ch):04X} at pos {pos}")

# Now do the REAL fix: send one INSERT at a time to isolate failures
# And use dollar-quoting instead of E'...' for problematic lines
def to_dollar_quoted(line):
    """Replace all '...' string literals with $$...$$ dollar-quoted equivalents."""
    # This avoids ALL encoding issues since dollar-quoted strings are literal
    result = []
    i = 0
    n = len(line)
    while i < n:
        if line[i] == "'":
            j = i + 1
            chars = []
            while j < n:
                if line[j] == "'" and j+1 < n and line[j+1] == "'":
                    chars.append("'"); j += 2  # unescape ''→'
                elif line[j] == "'":
                    j += 1; break
                else:
                    chars.append(line[j]); j += 1
            content_str = ''.join(chars)
            # Use unique dollar-quote tag to avoid collision
            tag = '$q$'
            result.append(f"{tag}{content_str}{tag}")
            i = j
        else:
            result.append(line[i]); i += 1
    return ''.join(result)

# Test on profile line 22 (0-indexed: 21)
if len(profile_inserts) >= 22:
    line22 = profile_inserts[21]
    dq = to_dollar_quoted(line22)
    print(f"\nOriginal line 22 (snippet): {line22[400:500]}")
    print(f"Dollar-quoted (snippet):   {dq[400:500]}")
    bad_after = [c for c in dq if ord(c)>127]
    print(f"Non-ASCII after dollar-quote: {len(bad_after)}")

python3 << 'PY'
import requests, json, re

PAT = "sbp_17a2f53bacd3a600904da2ce484de6fb830c32ea"
NEW_PROJECT = "jfizzduvmzavtqwzqacy"
URL = f"https://api.supabase.com/v1/projects/{NEW_PROJECT}/database/query"
HEADERS = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {PAT}", "Content-Type": "application/json"}

def to_dollar_quoted_insert(line):
    """Replace all '...' string literals with $q$...$q$ — avoids ALL encoding issues."""
    result = []
    i = 0
    n = len(line)
    while i < n:
        if line[i] == "'":
            j = i + 1
            chars = []
            while j < n:
                if line[j] == "'" and j+1 < n and line[j+1] == "'":
                    chars.append("'"); j += 2
                elif line[j] == "'":
                    j += 1; break
                else:
                    chars.append(line[j]); j += 1
            result.append("$q$" + "".join(chars) + "$q$")
            i = j
        else:
            result.append(line[i]); i += 1
    return "".join(result)

def send_one(sql, label):
    # Verify clean ASCII
    bad = [c for c in sql if ord(c) > 127]
    if bad:
        print(f"  ⚠️  {label}: {len(bad)} non-ASCII in SQL after transform!")
        return False
    payload = json.dumps({"query": sql}, ensure_ascii=True).encode("utf-8")
    resp = requests.post(URL, headers=HEADERS, data=payload, timeout=60)
    if resp.status_code in (200, 201):
        print(f"  ✅ {label}"); return True
    else:
        print(f"  ❌ {label}: {resp.text[:250]}"); return False

# Parse blocks from original SQL
with open('/tmp/public_ordered.sql', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
    content = f.read()

lines_all = content.split('\n')
blocks = {}
cur = None
for line in lines_all:
    if re.match(r'^-- \w', line) and '::' not in line and 'End of' not in line:
        cur = line[3:].strip()
        blocks[cur] = []
    elif cur:
        blocks[cur].append(line)

def import_table(name):
    inserts = [l.strip() for l in blocks.get(name, []) if l.strip().startswith('INSERT')]
    if not inserts:
        print(f"  ⏭️  {name}: no data"); return True
    # Convert each INSERT line individually, then batch
    converted = [to_dollar_quoted_insert(l) for l in inserts]
    sql = "\n".join(converted)
    return send_one(sql, name)

print("=== profiles + reels ===")
import_table('profiles')
import_table('reels')
print("\n=== reel_likes + reel_views (need reels) ===")
import_table('reel_likes')
import_table('reel_views')
PY

COMMAND:
python3 << 'PY'
# Debug: dollar-quoting still leaving non-ASCII — means non-ASCII chars are OUTSIDE string literals
# e.g. in comments or SQL keywords. Let's find them precisely.
import re

with open('/tmp/public_ordered.sql', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
    content = f.read()

lines_all = content.split('\n')
blocks = {}
cur = None
for line in lines_all:
    if re.match(r'^-- \w', line) and '::' not in line and 'End of' not in line:
        cur = line[3:].strip()
        blocks[cur] = []
    elif cur:
        blocks[cur].append(line)

def find_non_ascii_outside_strings(line):
    """Find non-ASCII chars that are OUTSIDE single-quoted strings."""
    outside = []
    i = 0
    n = len(line)
    in_string = False
    while i < n:
        if not in_string and line[i] == "'":
            in_string = True; i += 1
        elif in_string and line[i] == "'" and i+1 < n and line[i+1] == "'":
            i += 2  # escaped quote
        elif in_string and line[i] == "'":
            in_string = False; i += 1
        else:
            if not in_string and ord(line[i]) > 127:
                outside.append((i, line[i], hex(ord(line[i]))))
            i += 1
    return outside

for tname in ['profiles', 'reels']:
    inserts = [l.strip() for l in blocks.get(tname, []) if l.strip().startswith('INSERT')]
    print(f"\n{tname} ({len(inserts)} inserts):")
    for idx, line in enumerate(inserts):
        bad_outside = find_non_ascii_outside_strings(line)
        bad_total = [(i,c,h) for i,c,h in [(j,ch,hex(ord(ch))) for j,ch in enumerate(line)] if ord(c)>127]
        if bad_total:
            print(f"  Line {idx+1}: {len(bad_total)} non-ASCII total, {len(bad_outside)} outside strings")
            for pos,ch,hx in bad_outside[:3]:
                ctx = line[max(0,pos-15):pos+15]
                print(f"    OUTSIDE: '{ch}' ({hx}) context: {repr(ctx)}")
            # Show first non-ASCII inside string
            inside = [(p,c,h) for p,c,h in bad_total if (p,c,h) not in [(x,y,z) for x,y,z in bad_outside]]
            if inside:
                pos,ch,hx = inside[0]
                ctx = line[max(0,pos-15):pos+15]
                print(f"    INSIDE:  '{ch}' ({hx}) context: {repr(ctx)}")
PY

python3 << 'PY'
import requests, json, re

PAT = "sbp_17a2f53bacd3a600904da2ce484de6fb830c32ea"
NEW_PROJECT = "jfizzduvmzavtqwzqacy"
URL = f"https://api.supabase.com/v1/projects/{NEW_PROJECT}/database/query"
HEADERS = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {PAT}", "Content-Type": "application/json"}

# Root cause confirmed: all non-ASCII are INSIDE string literals
# Dollar-quoting ($q$...$q$) should work — but the check was wrong
# The "⚠️ non-ASCII" check ran BEFORE dollar-quoting. Let's fix the order.

def to_dollar_quoted_insert(line):
    """Replace all '...' SQL string literals with $q$...$q$."""
    result = []; i = 0; n = len(line)
    while i < n:
        if line[i] == "'":
            j = i + 1; chars = []
            while j < n:
                if line[j] == "'" and j+1 < n and line[j+1] == "'":
                    chars.append("'"); j += 2
                elif line[j] == "'":
                    j += 1; break
                else:
                    chars.append(line[j]); j += 1
            result.append("$q$" + "".join(chars) + "$q$"); i = j
        else:
            result.append(line[i]); i += 1
    return "".join(result)

def send_table(name, inserts):
    if not inserts:
        print(f"  ⏭️  {name}: no data"); return True
    converted = [to_dollar_quoted_insert(l) for l in inserts]
    sql = "\n".join(converted)
    # NOW check for non-ASCII (after conversion)
    bad = [c for c in sql if ord(c) > 127]
    if bad:
        print(f"  ❌ {name}: {len(bad)} non-ASCII after dollar-quote! First: {repr(bad[0][:20])}"); return False
    payload = json.dumps({"query": sql}, ensure_ascii=True).encode("utf-8")
    resp = requests.post(URL, headers=HEADERS, data=payload, timeout=60)
    if resp.status_code in (200, 201):
        print(f"  ✅ {name}"); return True
    else:
        print(f"  ❌ {name}: {resp.text[:250]}"); return False

# Parse blocks
with open('/tmp/public_ordered.sql', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
    content = f.read()

blocks = {}; cur = None
for line in content.split('\n'):
    if re.match(r'^-- \w', line) and '::' not in line and 'End of' not in line:
        cur = line[3:].strip(); blocks[cur] = []
    elif cur:
        blocks[cur].append(line)

def get_inserts(name):
    return [l.strip() for l in blocks.get(name, []) if l.strip().startswith('INSERT')]

print("=== profiles + reels ===")
ok_profiles = send_table('profiles', get_inserts('profiles'))
ok_reels = send_table('reels', get_inserts('reels'))

print("\n=== dependent tables ===")
if ok_reels:
    send_table('reel_likes', get_inserts('reel_likes'))
    send_table('reel_views', get_inserts('reel_views'))
else:
    print("  ⚠️  skipping reel_likes/reel_views — reels failed")
PY
